;(function(win, doc, style, timeout) {
  var STYLE_ID = 'at-body-style';

  function getParent() {
    return doc.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
  }

  function addStyle(parent, id, def) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.createElement('style');
    style.id = id;
    style.innerHTML = def;
    parent.appendChild(style);
  }

  function removeStyle(parent, id) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.getElementById(id);

    if (!style) {
      return;
    }

    parent.removeChild(style);
  }

  addStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID, style);
  setTimeout(function() {
    removeStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID);
  }, timeout);
}(window, document, "body {opacity: 0 !important}", 3000));
</script>

<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="richtext text cmp-bottom-spacing-40 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div data-icon-aria-label=" (opens in a new tab)" id="richtext-ef86886543" class="cmp-text rich-text-container">
    <div class="rich-text-wrapper">
        <h2 class="richtext--element-font"><span class="variant headlineLarge"><b>Frequently asked questions</b></span></h2>
 
        <!-- Used unsafe context as temporary fix and create adobe support ticket to
        figure out why html context adding extra ul and li elements -->
    </div>
</div>

    





    

</div>
<div class="accordion aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div>
    <dig-accordion data-cmp-multiple-expansion>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item0">
                How can I use artificial intelligence for audit workflows?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item0">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p><p>CoCounsel Audit &amp; Accounting leverages artificial intelligence to streamline and enhance your audit processes. Here are some key ways to use AI for audits:</p>
<ul><li><b>Tailored answers.</b> Get precise, authoritative responses to technical audit questions.</li><li><b>Document summarization.</b> Automatically compile and summarize essential information from client documents.</li><li><b>Scenario analysis.</b> Analyze complex client scenarios for actionable insights.</li><li><b>Research and compliance.</b> Conduct efficient audit and accounting research and review public company disclosures and accounting policies.</li><li><b>Agreement analysis.</b> Examine specific agreements, such as leases, revenue recognition, and warrants for compliance and accuracy.</li><li><b>Consistency and efficiency.</b> Ensure tasks are handled consistently with automated insights, reducing manual intervention.</li><li><b>Lease agreement evaluation.</b> Instantly review lease agreements, extract key terms, and flag potential issues.</li><li><b>Variable consideration clauses.</b> Identify and evaluate variable consideration clauses in SaaS contracts, highlighting specific clauses and ensuring compliance with ASC 606.</li></ul>
</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item1">
                How will AI improve audit engagements?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item1">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>CoCounsel improves audit engagements by providing verifiable outputs and streamlining real audit workflows. It automates repetitive tasks, such as analyzing trial balances, summarizing PDFs, and preparing risk assessments, allowing auditors to focus on higher-value activities and complex judgment calls. CoCounsel ensures consistency and reduces review cycles while integrating seamlessly with Excel and PDFs to support firm-approved processes and compliance with accounting standards like ASC 842 and ASC 606.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item2">
                Can CoCounsel help with audit planning and risk assessment?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item2">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes, CoCounsel can assist with audit planning by providing relevant regulatory guidance, historical data, and best practices. It can also help in risk assessment by analyzing financial statements and identifying potential areas of concern.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item3">
                Does CoCounsel support the creation and management of audit workpapers?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item3">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes, CoCounsel includes features for creating, organizing, and managing audit workpapers. It can automate the process of generating workpapers, ensuring they are accurate and compliant with regulatory standards.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item4">
                How does CoCounsel ensure the security and accuracy of audit processes?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item4">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>We’ve designed CoCounsel Audit &amp; Accounting with robust security measures to protect data, including being cloud-native and supporting remote workflows while ensuring data protection. For accuracy, it provides trustworthy, verifiable answers with plain-language explanations and citations from trusted sources such as Checkpoint, AICPA, FASB, GASB, firm templates, and audit methodologies. It integrates firm knowledge and supports real audit workflows, ensuring compliance with firm methodology and regulatory frameworks.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item5">
                Can firms that do not perform audits still use CoCounsel Audit &amp; Accounting for preparation, compilation, and review engagements?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item5">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes. Firms do not need to perform audits to benefit from CoCounsel Audit &amp; Accounting. Even firms focused only on preparation, compilation, review, tax, or advisory work still need reliable audit and assurance (A&amp;A) and preparation, compilation, and review (PCR) support. CoCounsel Audit &amp; Accounting helps firms answer technical accounting and Statements on Standards for Accounting and Review Services (SSARS) questions; draft and customize engagement letters, reports, representation letters, management letters, and client communications; analyze draft financial statements and workpapers; create disclosure checklists and note disclosures; and support staff training and standardization. It helps firms deliver non-audit services more efficiently, consistently, and confidently.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
    </dig-accordion>
</div>

    
</div>

                <script type="text/javascript">
// Hide OneTrust banner if wcmmode=disabled is present in URL or in PROD preview domain
if (window.location.search.includes('wcmmode=disabled') || window.location.host.includes('preview-prod-cs.ewp.thomsonreuters.com')) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = '[id*="onetrust"],[class*="onetrust"],[id*="ot-"],[class*="ot-"]{display:none!important}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
}

// function to pull cookie value
function getCookie(name) {
    var value = "; " + document.cookie;
    var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
    if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
}
function OptanonWrapper() { 
        setTimeout(() => {
            _satellite.pageBottom();
        }, 1000);
    var OABCcookieName = "OptanonConsent";
    var bannerAcceptBtn = document.getElementById("onetrust-accept-btn-handler");
    var pcAllowAllBtn = document.getElementById("accept-recommended-btn-handler");
    var pcSaveBtn = document.getElementsByClassName("save-preference-btn-handler onetrust-close-btn-handler")[0];
    var OABCcookie = getCookie(OABCcookieName);
        
   
    // IF logic needed here because ot-banner-sdk DIV is not injected on page loads if banner is not exposed
    if (!OABCcookie && bannerAcceptBtn) {
        bannerAcceptBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    }
    if (pcAllowAllBtn)
        pcAllowAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    if(pcSaveBtn){
        pcSaveBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            setTimeout(()=> {
            location.reload();
        }, 1000) //quick timeout so that the consent receipt can be sent and the cookie can be updated
        });
    }
} </script>
